    129                                                    if (data[7:0] != pattern[i]) begin
    130             1                    ***0***             `uvm_error("LOOPBACK", $sformatf("read 0x%02h expected 0x%02h", data[7:0], pattern[i]))
    131                                                    end
    132                                                  end

    165                                              class uart_irq_seq extends uart_base_apb_seq;
    166             1                    ***0***       `uvm_object_utils(uart_irq_seq)
    166             2                    ***0***
    166             3                    ***0***
    166             4                    ***0***
    166             5                    ***0***
    166             6                          3
    166             7                    ***0***
    166             8                    ***0***
    166             9                    ***0***
    166            10                    ***0***
    167
    168                                                function new(string name = "uart_irq_seq");
    169             1                          3         super.new(name);
    170                                                endfunction
    171
    172                                                task automatic check_irq_status(input bit exp_irq,
    173                                                                                input bit exp_rx_empty,
    174                                                                                input int unsigned idle_cycles,
    175                                                                                input string step_name);
    176                                                  bit [31:0] status;
    177
    178             1                         24         apb_read(ADDR_STATUS, status, idle_cycles);
    179                                                  if (status[4] != exp_irq || status[2] != exp_rx_empty) begin
    180                                                    `uvm_error("IRQ_STATUS",
    181                                                               $sformatf("%s: STATUS=0x%08h, expected irq=%0b rx_empty=%0b",
    182             1                    ***0***                                step_name, status, exp_irq, exp_rx_empty))
    183                                                  end
    184                                                endtask
    185
    186                                                task body();
    187                                                  bit [31:0] data;
    188
    189             1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_BAUD, 32'd1, 1);
    190
    191                                                  // Keep loopback enabled while IRQ is disabled. The received byte must stay
    192                                                  // in the FIFO without asserting the interrupt.
    193             1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_CTRL, 32'h3, 4);
    194             1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b0, 1'b1, 4, "reset state with IRQ disabled");
    195             1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_TXDATA, 32'ha5, 2);
    196             1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b0, 1'b0, 360, "RX pending with IRQ disabled");
    197
    198                                                  // Enabling IRQ while data is already pending must assert it immediately.
    199             1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_CTRL, 32'h7, 2);
    200             1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b1, 1'b0, 2, "enable IRQ with pending data");
    201             1                          3         apb_read(ADDR_RXDATA, data, 2);
    202                                                  if (data[7:0] != 8'ha5) begin
    203             1                    ***0***           `uvm_error("IRQ_DATA", $sformatf("read 0x%02h expected 0xa5", data[7:0]))
    204                                                  end
    205             1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b0, 1'b1, 8, "FIFO empty after first read");
    206
    207                                                  // With IRQ enabled, a new RX byte must assert it. Disabling IRQ must lower
    208                                                  // the output without consuming the pending byte.
    209             1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_TXDATA, 32'h3c, 2);
    210             1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b1, 1'b0, 360, "new RX data with IRQ enabled");
    211             1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_CTRL, 32'h3, 2);
    212             1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b0, 1'b0, 2, "disable IRQ with pending data");
    213             1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_CTRL, 32'h7, 2);
    214             1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b1, 1'b0, 2, "re-enable IRQ with pending data");
    215
    216             1                          3         apb_read(ADDR_RXDATA, data, 2);
    217                                                  if (data[7:0] != 8'h3c) begin
    218             1                    ***0***           `uvm_error("IRQ_DATA", $sformatf("read 0x%02h expected 0x3c", data[7:0]))
    219                                                  end
    220             1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b0, 1'b1, 8, "FIFO empty after second read");
    221                                                endtask
    222                                              endclass

    170                                                  uart_reset_recovery_seq         recovery_seq;

    173             1                          3         midflight_seq = uart_reset_midflight_seq::type_id::create("midflight_seq");
    174             1                          3         defaults_seq  = uart_reset_defaults_seq::type_id::create("defaults_seq");
    175             1                          3         preserved_seq = uart_reset_preserved_config_seq::type_id::create("preserved_seq");
    176             1                          3         recovery_seq  = uart_reset_recovery_seq::type_id::create("recovery_seq");
    177
    178                                                  // Reset both domains while a TX byte is pending, then prove clean recovery.
    179             1                          3         midflight_seq.start(env.apb.seqr);
    180             1                          3         reset_vif.pulse_both(3, 3);
    181             1                          3         defaults_seq.start(env.apb.seqr);
    182             1                          3         recovery_seq.start(env.apb.seqr);
    183
    184                                                  // APB-only reset clears APB configuration and both FIFO pointer domains.
    185             1                          3         reset_vif.pulse_apb_reset(3);
    186             1                          3         defaults_seq.start(env.apb.seqr);
    187             1                          3         recovery_seq.start(env.apb.seqr);
    188
    189                                                  // UART-only reset keeps APB configuration but flushes both FIFOs.
    190             1                          3         reset_vif.pulse_uart_reset(3);
    191             1                          3         preserved_seq.start(env.apb.seqr);
    192             1                          3         recovery_seq.start(env.apb.seqr);

    195             1                          3         phase.drop_objection(this);

    199                                              class uart_baud_timing_test extends uart_base_test;
    200             1                    ***0***       `uvm_component_utils(uart_baud_timing_test)
    200             2                    ***0***
    200             3                          3
    201
    202                                                virtual uart_if timing_vif;
    203                                                bit [31:0] cov_baud_programmed;
    204                                                bit        cov_timing_pass;
    205                                                realtime   uart_period;
    206
    207                                                covergroup baud_timing_cg;
    208                                                  option.per_instance = 1;
    209
    210                                                  cp_baud: coverpoint cov_baud_programmed {
    211                                                    bins zero  = {32'd0};
    212                                                    bins one   = {32'd1};
    213                                                    bins four  = {32'd4};
    214                                                    bins eight = {32'd8};
    215                                                  }
    216
    217                                                  cp_result: coverpoint cov_timing_pass {
    218                                                    bins pass = {1'b1};
    219                                                    illegal_bins fail = {1'b0};
    220                                                  }
    221
    222                                                  cross cp_baud, cp_result;
    223                                                endgroup
    224
    225                                                function new(string name = "uart_baud_timing_test", uvm_component parent = null);
    226             1                          3         super.new(name, parent);
    227             1                          3         baud_timing_cg = new();
    228                                                endfunction
    229
    230                                                function void build_phase(uvm_phase phase);
    231             1                          3         super.build_phase(phase);
    232                                                  if (!uvm_config_db#(virtual uart_if)::get(this, "", "timing_vif", timing_vif)) begin
    233             1                    ***0***           `uvm_fatal("NOTIMINGVIF", "UART timing interface is not set")
    234                                                  end
    235                                                endfunction
    236
    237                                                task automatic measure_frame(input bit [31:0] programmed_baud);
    238                                                  realtime last_edge;
    239                                                  realtime this_edge;
    240                                                  realtime measured;
    241                                                  realtime expected;
    242                                                  realtime difference;
    243                                                  int unsigned effective_baud;
    244
    245             1                         12         effective_baud = (programmed_baud == 0) ? 1 : programmed_baud;
    246             1                         12         expected = uart_period * effective_baud;
    247             1                         12         cov_timing_pass = 1'b1;
    248
    249                                                  // 0x55 changes level at every frame bit boundary. Starting at the falling
    250                                                  // start-bit edge gives nine independent bit-width measurements.
    251             1                         12         @(negedge timing_vif.tx_o);
    252             1                         12         last_edge = $realtime;
    253             1                         12         for (int i = 0; i < 9; i++) begin
    253             2                        108
    254             1                        108           @(timing_vif.tx_o);
    255             1                        108           this_edge = $realtime;
    256             1                        108           measured = this_edge - last_edge;
    257                                                    difference = (measured > expected) ? (measured - expected)
    258             1                        108                                              : (expected - measured);
    259                                                    if (difference > 1ps) begin
    260             1                    ***0***             cov_timing_pass = 1'b0;
    261                                                      `uvm_error("BAUD_TIMING",
    262                                                                 $sformatf("BAUD=%0d bit=%0d measured=%0t expected=%0t",
    263             1                    ***0***                                  programmed_baud, i, measured, expected))
    264                                                    end
    265             1                        108           last_edge = this_edge;
    266                                                  end
    267
    268             1                         12         cov_baud_programmed = programmed_baud;
    269             1                         12         baud_timing_cg.sample();
    270                                                  `uvm_info("BAUD_TIMING",
    271                                                            $sformatf("BAUD=%0d effective=%0d measured bit width=%0t",
    272                                                                      programmed_baud, effective_baud, expected),
    273             1                         12                   UVM_LOW)
    274                                                endtask
    275
    276                                                task automatic run_baud_case(input bit [31:0] programmed_baud);
    277                                                  uart_baud_tx_seq seq;
    278             1                         12         seq = uart_baud_tx_seq::type_id::create($sformatf("baud_%0d_seq", programmed_baud));
    279             1                         12         seq.baud_value = programmed_baud;
    280                                                  fork
    281             1                         12           seq.start(env.apb.seqr);
    282             1                         12           measure_frame(programmed_baud);
    283                                                  join
    284                                                endtask
    285
    286                                                task run_phase(uvm_phase phase);
    287                                                  realtime first_edge;
    288
    289             1                          3         phase.raise_objection(this);
    290             1                          3         @(posedge timing_vif.uart_clk);
    291             1                          3         first_edge = $realtime;
    292             1                          3         @(posedge timing_vif.uart_clk);
    293             1                          3         uart_period = $realtime - first_edge;
    294
    295             1                          3         run_baud_case(32'd0);
    296             1                          3         run_baud_case(32'd1);
    297             1                          3         run_baud_case(32'd4);
    298             1                          3         run_baud_case(32'd8);
    299
    300             1                          3         #1us;
    301             1                          3         phase.drop_objection(this);
    302                                                endtask
    303                                              endclass
